## Narration 101

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A101)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَائِشَةَ (companion). The answer is explicitly attributed to Aisha; it is not presented as direct Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ، سُئِلَتْ عَنْ غُسْلِ الْمَرْأَةِ، مِنَ الْجَنَابَةِ فَقَالَتْ لِتَحْفِنْ عَلَى رَأْسِهَا ثَلاَثَ حَفَنَاتٍ مِنَ الْمَاءِ وَلْتَضْغَثْ رَأْسَهَا بِيَدَيْهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 101]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The answer is explicitly attributed to Aisha; it is not presented as direct Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that A&#x27;isha was asked about how a woman should do ghusl for major ritual impurity. She said, &quot;She should scoop water over her head with both hands three times and rub the roots of her hair with her hands</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 101]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से रिवायत की, इमाम मालिक से के उन्होंने सुना के आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से पूछा गया के औरत को गुस्ल-ए-जनाबत कैसे करना चाहिए। उन्होंने फ़रमाया: उसे दोनों हाथों से तीन बार अपने सर पर पानी डालना चाहिए और अपने हाथों से बालों की जड़ों को मलना चाहिए।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے روایت کی، امام مالک سے کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا سے پوچھا گیا کہ عورت کو غسلِ جنابت کیسے کرنا چاہیے۔ انہوں نے فرمایا: اسے دونوں ہاتھوں سے تین بار اپنے سر پر پانی ڈالنا چاہیے اور اپنے ہاتھوں سے بالوں کی جڑوں کو ملنا چاہیے۔</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se riwayat ki, Imam Malik se ke unhon ne suna ke A&#x27;isha (Razi Allahu Anha) se poocha gaya ke aurat ko ghusl-e-janabat kaise karna chahiye. Unhon ne farmaya: Use dono hathon se teen baar apne sar par pani daalna chahiye aur apne hathon se baalon ki jadon ko malna chahiye.</p>

## Narration 102

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A102)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، وَعُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ، وَعَائِشَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانُوا يَقُولُونَ إِذَا مَسَّ الْخِتَانُ الْخِتَانَ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الْغُسْلُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 102]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/102" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 102</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   102 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab that Umar ibn al-Khattab and Uthman ibn Affan and A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to say, &quot;When the circumcised part touches the circumcised part, ghusl is obligatory</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 102]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/102" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 102</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   102 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रदि अल्लाहु अन्हु), हज़रत उस्मान बिन अफ़्फ़ान (रदि अल्लाहु अन्हु), और हज़रत आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) का क़ौल यही था के जब ख़तना, ख़तने से मिल जाए यानी ज़िक्र का सिर औरत की शरमगाह में ग़ैब हो जाए, तो ग़ुस्ल वाजिब हो जाता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 102]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/102" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن المسیب سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر بن خطاب اور حضرت عثمان بن عفان اور حضرت عائشہ کا قول یہی تھا کہ جب مس کرے ختنہ ختنہ سے یعنی سر ذکر عورت کی قبل میں غائب ہو جائے تو واجب ہوا غسل ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 102]</p>

## Narration 103

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A103)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَائِشَةَ (companion). The narrated exchange preserves Aisha&#x27;s response to Abu Salama. The Bengali footnote call remains as printed and explanatory insertions are kept separately.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سَأَلْتُ عَائِشَةَ زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَا يُوجِبُ الْغُسْلَ فَقَالَتْ هَلْ تَدْرِي مَا مَثَلُكَ يَا أَبَا سَلَمَةَ مَثَلُ الْفَرُّوجِ يَسْمَعُ الدِّيَكَةَ تَصْرُخُ فَيَصْرُخُ مَعَهَا إِذَا جَاوَزَ الْخِتَانُ الْخِتَانَ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الْغُسْلُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 103]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/103" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The narrated exchange preserves Aisha&#x27;s response to Abu Salama. The Bengali footnote call remains as printed and explanatory insertions are kept separately.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;n Nadr, the mawla of Umar ibn Abdullah that Abu Salamaibn Abdar-Rahman ibn Awf related that he had asked A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, what made ghusl obligatory. She said, &quot;Do you know what you are like, Abu Salama? You are like a chick when it hears the cocks crowing and so crows with them. When the circumcised part passes the circumcised part, ghusl is obligatory</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 103]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/103" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबी सलामा बिन अब्दुर रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के मैंने हज़रत आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से पूछा के किस चीज़ से गुस्ल वाजिब होता है? तोह हज़रत आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;ऐ अबू सलामा! तुम अपनी सिफ़त को जानते हो, तुम्हारी सिफ़त मुर्ग़ के चूज़े की तरह है, जब मुर्ग़ को बांग करते सुनता है तोह आप भी बांग करने लगता है. जब ख़तना, ख़तने से तजावुज़ कर जाए तोह गुस्ल वाजिब हो जाता है.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/103" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عائشہ (companion). The narrated exchange preserves Aisha&#x27;s response to Abu Salama. The Bengali footnote call remains as printed and explanatory insertions are kept separately.</p>

<p dir="rtl">ابی سلمہ بن عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ میں نے پوچھا حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا کس چیز سے غسل واجب ہوتا ہے تو کہا حضرت عائشہ نے کہ تو جانتا ہے اپنی صفت کو اے ابوسلمہ صفت تیری مثل چوزہ مرغ کے ہے جب مرغ کو بانگ کرتے سنتا ہے تو آپ بھی بانگ کرنے لگتا ہے جب تجاوز کرے ختنہ ختنے سے تو واجب ہوا غسل ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 103]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abi Salama bin Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke maine Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se pucha ke kis cheez se ghusal wajib hota hai? Toh Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) ne farmaya: &#x27;Aye Abu Salama! Tum apni sifat ko jante ho, tumhari sifat murg ke chuzze ki tarah hai, jab murg ko bang karte sunta hai toh aap bhi bang karne lagta hai. Jab khatna, khatne se tajawuz kar jaye toh ghusal wajib ho jata hai.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/103" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 104

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A104)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ أَبَا مُوسَى الأَشْعَرِيَّ، أَتَى عَائِشَةَ زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ لَهَا لَقَدْ شَقَّ عَلَىَّ اخْتِلاَفُ أَصْحَابِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي أَمْرٍ إِنِّي لأُعْظِمُ أَنْ أَسْتَقْبِلَكِ بِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ مَا هُوَ مَا كُنْتَ سَائِلاً عَنْهُ أُمَّكَ فَسَلْنِي عَنْهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الرَّجُلُ يُصِيبُ أَهْلَهُ ثُمَّ يُكْسِلُ وَلاَ يُنْزِلُ فَقَالَتْ إِذَا جَاوَزَ الْخِتَانُ الْخِتَانَ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الْغُسْلُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَبُو مُوسَى الأَشْعَرِيُّ لاَ أَسْأَلُ عَنْ هَذَا أَحَدًا بَعْدَكِ أَبَدًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Said ibn al-Musayyab that Abu Musa al-Ashari came to A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said to her, &quot;The disagreement of the companions in a matter which I hate to bring before you has distressed me.&quot; She said, &quot;What is that? You did not ask your mother about it, so ask me.&quot; He said, &quot;A man penetrates his wife, but becomes listless and does not ejaculate. &quot;She said, &quot;When the circumcised part passes the circumcised part ghusl is obligatory.&quot; Abu Musa added, &quot;I shall never ask anyone about this after you</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के अबू मूसा ऐश&#x27;आरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) हज़रात आयेशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास आये और कहा के मुझे सहाबा-इ-रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का एक मसले में इख्तिलाफ बहुत सख्त गुज़रा है और मैं शर्माता हूँ कि तुम्हारे सामने ज़िक्र करून. तोह आयेशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;वह क्या मसला है जो तुम अपनी माँ से न पूछ सको?&#x27; अबू मूसा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: &#x27;कोई जमा करता है अपनी बीवी से और दुखूल करता है मगर इंज़ाल (डिस्चार्ज) नहीं होता तोह घुसल वाजिब हो जाता है.&#x27; अबू मूसा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: &#x27;अब मैं तुम्हारे बाद ये मसला किसी से नहीं पूछूंगा.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ ابوموسیٰ اشعری آئے حضرت عائشہ کے پاس اور کہا ان سے کہ بہت سخت گزرا مجھ کو اختلاف صحابہ رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کا ایک مسئلے میں شرماتا ہوں کہ ذکر کروں اس کو تمہارے سامنے تو فرمایا عائشہ نے کہ کیا ہے وہ مسئلہ جو تو اپنی ماں سے پوچھ لے مجھ سے کہا ابوموسیٰ نے کوئی جماع کرے اپنی بیوی سے پھر دخول کرے لیکن انزال نہ ہو تو کیا حکم ہے اس نے کہا کہ جب تجاوز کر جائے ختنہ ختنے سے واجب ہوا غسل کہا ابوموسیٰ نے کہ اب نہ پوچھوں گا اس مسئلے کو کسی سے بعد تمہارے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyab se riwayat hai ke Abu Musa Ash&#x27;ari (Radi Allahu Anhu) Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) ke paas aaye aur kaha ke mujhe Sahaba-e-Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ka ek masle mein ikhtilaf bahut sakht guzra hai aur main sharmata hoon ke tumhare samne zikr karoon. Toh Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) ne farmaya: &#x27;Woh kya masla hai jo tum apni maa se na puch sako?&#x27; Abu Musa (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: &#x27;Koi jama karta hai apni biwi se aur dukhool karta hai magar inzal (discharge) nahi hota, toh kya hukm hai?&#x27; Aap (Radi Allahu Anha) ne kaha: &#x27;Jab khatna, khatne se tajawuz kar jaye toh ghusal wajib ho jata hai.&#x27; Abu Musa (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: &#x27;Ab main tumhare baad ye masla kisi se nahi puchunga.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">

## Narration 105

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A105)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: مَحْمُودَ بْنَ لَبِيدٍ الأَنْصَارِيَّ (companion); زَيْدَ بْنَ ثَابِتٍ (companion); أُبَىَّ بْنَ كَعْبٍ (companion). The source transmits Mahmud&#x27;s question and Zayd&#x27;s answer concerning Ubayy&#x27;s reported earlier opinion. The historical claim remains attributed to its source speaker.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ، مَوْلَى عُثْمَانَ بْنِ عَفَّانَ أَنَّ مَحْمُودَ بْنَ لَبِيدٍ الأَنْصَارِيَّ، سَأَلَ زَيْدَ بْنَ ثَابِتٍ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ، يُصِيبُ أَهْلَهُ ثُمَّ يُكْسِلُ وَلاَ يُنْزِلُ فَقَالَ زَيْدٌ يَغْتَسِلُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ مَحْمُودٌ إِنَّ أُبَىَّ بْنَ كَعْبٍ كَانَ لاَ يَرَى الْغُسْلَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ إِنَّ أُبَىَّ بْنَ كَعْبٍ نَزَعَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَمُوتَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 105]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Mahmud ibn Labid al-Ansari (companion); Zayd ibn Thabit (companion); Ubayy ibn Kab (companion). The source transmits Mahmud&#x27;s question and Zayd&#x27;s answer concerning Ubayy&#x27;s reported earlier opinion. The historical claim remains attributed to its source speaker.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Abdullah ibn Kab, the mawla of Uthman ibn Affan that Mahmud ibn Labid al-Ansari asked Zayd ibn Thabit about a man who penetrated his wife but became listless and did not ejaculate. Zayd ibn Thabit said, &quot;He does ghusl.&quot; Mahmud said to him, &quot;Ubayy ibn Kab used not to think that ghusl was necessary,&quot; but Zayd ibn Thabit said, &quot;Ubayy ibn Kab drew away from that before he died</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 105]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">महमूद बिन लॉबीद अंसारी ने ज़ैद बिन थाबित अंसारी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पूछा के एक शख्स अपनी बीवी से जमा करता है और दुखूल करता है मगर इंज़ाल नहीं होता? ज़ैद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: &#x27;घुसल करे.&#x27; महमूद ने कहा: &#x27;उबय्य बिन का&#x27;अब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) इस सूरत में घुसल को वाजिब नहीं जानते थे.&#x27; ज़ैद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: &#x27;उबय्य बिन का&#x27;अब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) मौत से पहले इस क़ौल से फिर गए थे.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

The source transmits Mahmud&#x27;s question and Zayd&#x27;s answer concerning Ubayy&#x27;s reported earlier opinion. The historical claim remains attributed to its source speaker.</p>

<p dir="rtl">محمود بن لبید انصاری نے پوچھا زید بن ثابت انصاری سے کہا ایک شخص جماع کرے اپنی بیوی سے پھر دخول کرے لیکن انزال نہ ہو کہا زید نے غسل کرے کہا محمود نے کہ ابی بن کعب اس صورت میں غسل کو واجب نہیں جانتے تھے کہا زید نے کہ ابی بن کعب قبل موت کے پھر گئے اس قول سے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 105]</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Mahmood bin Labeed Ansari ne Zaid bin Thabit Ansari (Radi Allahu Anhu) se pucha ke ek shakhs apni biwi se jama karta hai aur dukhool karta hai magar inzal nahi hota? Zaid (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: &#x27;Ghusal kare.&#x27; Mahmood ne kaha: &#x27;Ubayy bin Ka&#x27;ab (Radi Allahu Anhu) is surat mein ghusal ko wajib nahi jante the.&#x27; Zaid (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: &#x27;Ubayy bin Ka&#x27;ab (Radi Allahu Anhu) maut se pehle is qaul se phir gaye the.&#x27;</p>

## Narration 106

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A106)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). This is Ibn Umar&#x27;s attributed statement; the source does not make it direct Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ إِذَا جَاوَزَ الْخِتَانُ الْخِتَانَ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الْغُسْلُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 106]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/106" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;When the circumcised part passes the circumcised part, ghusl is obligatory</p>

## Narration 107

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A107)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ ذَكَرَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ يُصِيبُهُ جَنَابَةٌ مِنَ اللَّيْلِ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ‏ &quot;‏ تَوَضَّأْ وَاغْسِلْ ذَكَرَكَ ثُمَّ نَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 107]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 107</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   107 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Dinar that Abdullah ibn Umar related that Umar ibn al-Khattab mentioned to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that he would sometimes become junub in the night. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;Do wudu and wash your penis, and then sleep</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 107]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   107 ]</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से ज़िक्र किया कि उन्हें रात को नहाने की हाजत होती है फ़रमाया: &#x27;वुज़ू कर लो और अपना ज़िक्र धो लो</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 107]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر نے ذکر کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہ اسے رات کو نہانے کی حاجت ہوتی ہے تو فرمایا آنحضرت صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے وضو کرلے اور دھولے ذکر اپنے کو پھر سو جائے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 107]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se zikr kiya ke unhe raat ko nahanay ki haajat hoti hai, toh Aan-Hazrat (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: &#x27;Wuzu kar lo aur apna zikr dho lo, phir so jao.&#x27;</p>

## Narration 108

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A108)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَائِشَةَ (companion). The source attributes this instruction to Aisha. Exact supplied wording is preserved without reconstructing a missing or altered expression.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا كَانَتْ تَقُولُ إِذَا أَصَابَ أَحَدُكُمُ الْمَرْأَةَ ثُمَّ أَرَادَ أَنْ يَنَامَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَغْتَسِلَ فَلاَ يَنَمْ حَتَّى يَتَوَضَّأَ وُضُوءَهُ لِلصَّلاَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 108]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The source attributes this instruction to Aisha. Exact supplied wording is preserved without reconstructing a missing or altered expression.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to say, &quot;If you have intercourse with your wife and then wish to go to sleep before doing ghusl, do not sleep until you have done wudu as for prayer</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 108]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عائشہ (companion). The source attributes this instruction to Aisha. Exact supplied wording is preserved without reconstructing a missing or altered expression.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ وہ کہتی تھیں جب کوئی تم میں سے جماع کرے اپنی عورت سے پھر سونا چاہے قبل غسل کرے تو یہ سوئے یہاں تک کہ وضو کر لے جیسے کہ وضو ہوتا ہے نماز کے لئے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 108]</p>

## Narration 109

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A109)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). The source reports Ibn Umar&#x27;s practice. The next English section heading is retained as source context rather than quotation text.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ إِذَا أَرَادَ أَنْ يَنَامَ، أَوْ يَطْعَمَ وَهُوَ جُنُبٌ غَسَلَ وَجْهَهُ وَيَدَيْهِ إِلَى الْمِرْفَقَيْنِ وَمَسَحَ بِرَأْسِهِ ثُمَّ طَعِمَ أَوْ نَامَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 109]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/109" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 109</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   109 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source reports Ibn Umar&#x27;s practice. The next English section heading is retained as source context rather than quotation text.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar, if he wished to sleep or eat while junub, would wash his face, and his arms to the elbows, and wipe his head. Then he would eat or sleep. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 109]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/109" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عبداللہ بن عمر (companion). The source reports Ibn Umar&#x27;s practice. The next English section heading is retained as source context rather than quotation text.</p>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ وہ جب سو رہنے یا کھانے کا ارادہ رکھتے حالت جنابت میں منہ دھوتے اور دونوں ہاتھ کہنیوں تک اور سر پر مسح کرتے پھر کھانا کھاتے یا سو رہتے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 109]</p>

## Narration 110

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A110)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي حَكِيمٍ، أَنَّ عَطَاءَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَبَّرَ فِي صَلاَةٍ مِنَ الصَّلَوَاتِ ثُمَّ أَشَارَ إِلَيْهِمْ بِيَدِهِ أَنِ امْكُثُوا فَذَهَبَ ثُمَّ رَجَعَ وَعَلَى جِلْدِهِ أَثَرُ الْمَاءِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 110]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 110</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   110 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ismail ibn Abi Hakim that Ata ibn Yasar told him that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said the takbir in one of the prayers and then indicated to them with his hand to stay in place. He left and then returned with traces of water on his skin</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 110]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 110</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   110 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अत बिन यसर से रिवायत है के आँ-हज़रात (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने किसी नमाज़ में तक्बीर कही और फिर मुक़्तदीयों को अपने हाथ से इशारा किया कि अपनी जगह पर जमे रहो. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) घर गए और बाद में लौट कर आये तोह आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के बदन पर पानी के निशान थे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 110]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 110</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   110 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عطا بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ آنحضرت صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے تکبیر کہی کسی نماز میں نمازوں میں سے پھر اشارہ کیا مقتدیوں کو اپنے ہاتھ سے اس بات کا کہ اپنی جائے پر جمے رہو اور آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم گئے گھر میں بعد اس کے لوٹ کر آئے اور آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے بدن پر پانی کے نشان تھے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 110]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ata bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Aan-Hazrat (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne kisi namaz mein takbeer kahi aur phir muqtadiyon ko apne hath se ishara kiya ke apni jagah par jame raho. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ghar gaye aur baad mein laut kar aaye toh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke badan par pani ke nishan the.</p>

## Narration 111

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A111)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ زُيَيْدِ بْنِ الصَّلْتِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ خَرَجْتُ مَعَ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ إِلَى الْجُرُفِ فَنَظَرَ فَإِذَا هُوَ قَدِ احْتَلَمَ وَصَلَّى وَلَمْ يَغْتَسِلْ فَقَالَ وَاللَّهِ مَا أَرَانِي إِلاَّ احْتَلَمْتُ وَمَا شَعَرْتُ وَصَلَّيْتُ وَمَا اغْتَسَلْتُ قَالَ فَاغْتَسَلَ وَغَسَلَ مَا رَأَى فِي ثَوْبِهِ وَنَضَحَ مَا لَمْ يَرَ وَأَذَّنَ أَوْ أَقَامَ ثُمَّ صَلَّى بَعْدَ ارْتِفَاعِ الضُّحَى مُتَمَكِّنًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 111]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that Zuyayd ibn as-Salt said, &quot;I went with Umar ibn al-Khattab to Juruf and he looked down and noticed that he had had a wet dream and had prayed without doing ghusl. He exclaimed, &#x27;By Allah I realise that I have had a wet dream and did not know it and have not done ghusl.&#x27; So he did ghusl and washed off whatever he saw on his garment, and sprinkled with water whatever he did not see.Then he gave the adhan or the iqama and prayed in the midmorning</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 111]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ुबैद बिन साल्ट से रिवायत है के मैं उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के साथ जर्फ तक निकला, तोह उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने अपने कपड़ों को देखा और एहतलाम (wet dream) का निशान पाया, हालाँकि वोह बग़ैर गुस्ल किए नमाज़ पढ़ चुके थे. तब उन्होंने कहा: अल्लाह की क़सम, मैं अपने आप को देख रहा हूँ मगर मुझे एहतलाम हुआ और ख़बर न हुई, फिर नमाज़ पढ़ ली और गुस्ल नहीं किया. ज़ुबैद ने कहा: फिर हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने गुस्ल किया, और जो निशान कपड़े में दिखाई दिया उससे धोया और जहाँ निशान नहीं दिखा वहाँ पानी छिड़क दिया, फिर अज़ान या इक़ामत कही और सूरज बुलंद होने पर इत्मीनान से नमाज़ पढ़ी.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عمر بن خطاب (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="rtl">زیبد بن صلت سے روایت ہے کہ نکلا میں ساتھ عمر بن خطاب کے جرف تک تو دیکھا عمر نے اپنے کپڑے کو اور پایا نشان احتلام کا اور نماز پڑھ چکے تھے بغیر غسل کے تب کہا قسم اللہ کی نہیں دیکھتا ہوں میں اپنے کو مگر مجھے احتلام ہوا اور خبر نہ ہوئی اور نماز پڑھ لی اور غسل نہیں کیا کہا زبید نے پس غسل کیا حضرت عمر نے اور دھویا جو نشان دکھائی دیا کپڑے میں اور جو نہ دکھائی دیا اس پر پانی چھڑک دیا اور اذان کہی یا اقامت کہی پھر نماز پڑھی جب آفتاب بلند ہو گیا اطمینان سے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 111]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   111 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zubaid bin Salt se riwayat hai ke main Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke saath Jarf tak nikla, toh Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne apne kapdon ko dekha aur ihtilam (wet dream) ka nishan paya, halanki woh baghair ghusl kiye namaz parh chuke thay. Tab unhone kaha: Allah ki qasam, main apne aap ko dekh raha hoon magar mujhe ihtilam hua aur khabar na hui, phir namaz parh li aur ghusl nahi kiya. Zubaid ne kaha: Phir Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ghusl kiya, aur jo nishan kapde mein dikhayi diya usse dhoya aur jahan nishan nahi dikha wahan pani chhirak diya, phir azan ya iqamat kahi aur suraj buland hone par itminan se namaz parhi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/111" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 112

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A112)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي حَكِيمٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، غَدَا إِلَى أَرْضِهِ بِالْجُرُفِ فَوَجَدَ فِي ثَوْبِهِ احْتِلاَمًا فَقَالَ لَقَدِ ابْتُلِيتُ بِالاِحْتِلاَمِ مُنْذُ وُلِّيتُ أَمْرَ النَّاسِ ‏.‏ فَاغْتَسَلَ وَغَسَلَ مَا رَأَى فِي ثَوْبِهِ مِنَ الاِحْتِلاَمِ ثُمَّ صَلَّى بَعْدَ أَنْ طَلَعَتِ الشَّمْسُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 112]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/112" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ismail ibn Abi Hakim from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Umar ibn al-Khattab went out early in the morning to his land in al-Juruf and found semen on his garment. He said, &quot;I have been tried with wet dreams since I have been entrusted with governing the people.&quot; He did ghusl and washed his garment of what he saw of the semen, and then prayed after the sun had risen</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 112]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/112" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसर से रिवायत है के उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) सुबह अपनी ज़मीन पर गए जो जर्फ़ में थी तोह अपने कपडे में एह्तिलाम का निशान देखा. फिर कहा: मैं जब से खलीफा बना हूँ हूँ. फिर ग़ुस्ल किया और कपडे में पाया गया एह्तिलाम का निशान धोया और सूरज निकल आने पर नमाज़ पढ़ी.</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عمر بن خطاب (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ عمر بن خطاب صبح کو گئے اپنی زمین میں جو جرف میں تھی پس دیکھا اپنے کپڑے میں نشان احتلام کا پھر کہا میں مبتلا ہو گیا احتلام میں جب سے خلیفہ ہوا پھر غسل کیا اور دھویا جو نشان پایا اپنے کپڑے میں احتلام کا پھر نماز پڑھی جب آفتاب نکل آیا</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) subah apni zameen par gaye jo Jarf mein thi, toh apne kapde mein ihtilam ka nishan dekha. Phir kaha: Main jab se khalifa bana hoon, ihtilam mein mubtala ho gaya hoon. Phir ghusl kiya aur kapde mein paya gaya ihtilam ka nishan dhoya aur suraj nikal aane par namaz parhi.</p>

## Narration 113

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A113)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، صَلَّى بِالنَّاسِ الصُّبْحَ ثُمَّ غَدَا إِلَى أَرْضِهِ بِالْجُرُفِ فَوَجَدَ فِي ثَوْبِهِ احْتِلاَمًا فَقَالَ إِنَّا لَمَّا أَصَبْنَا الْوَدَكَ لاَنَتِ الْعُرُوقُ ‏.‏ فَاغْتَسَلَ وَغَسَلَ الاِحْتِلاَمَ مِنْ ثَوْبِهِ وَعَادَ لِصَلاَتِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 113]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/113" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Umar ibn al-Khattab led the people in the subh prayer and then went out to his land in Juruf and found semen on his clothes. He said, &quot;Since we have been eating rich meat our veins have become fulsome.&quot; He did ghusl, washed the semen from his clothing, and did his prayer again</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 113]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/113" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यासार से रिवायत है के उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने लोगों को सुबह की नमाज़ पढ़ाई, फिर अपनी ज़मीन की तरफ गए जो जरफ में थी। वहां अपने कपड़े में एहतलाम का निशान देखा तो कहा: जब से हम चर्बी खाने लगे हैं, रगें नर्म हो गई हैं। फिर गुस्ल किया और कपड़े से एहतलाम का निशान धोया और नमाज़ दोहराई।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/113" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عمر بن خطاب (companion). The source narrates Umar&#x27;s words and subsequent actions. The exact statement and language-specific details remain attributed to Umar; translator glosses stay in Comments where explicit.</p>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ عمر بن خطاب نے صبح کی نماز پڑھائی لوگوں کو پھر گئے اپنی زمین کی طرف جو جرف میں تھی پس دیکھا اپنے کپڑے میں نشان احتلام کا تو کہا کہ جب سے ہم کھانے لگے چربی نرم ہوگئیں رگیں پھر غسل کیا اور دھویا احتلام کے نشان کو اپنے کپڑے سے اور لوٹا یا نماز کو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 113]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne logon ko subah ki namaz parhayi, phir apni zameen ki taraf gaye jo Jarf mein thi. Wahan apne kapde mein ihtilam ka nishan dekha toh kaha: Jab se hum charbi khane lage hain, ragen narm ho gayi hain. Phir ghusl kiya aur kapde se ihtilam ka nishan dhoya aur namaz dohrayi.</p>

## Narration 114

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A114)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ (companion); عَمْرُو بْنُ الْعَاصِ (companion). The narrated journey and exchange between Umar and Amr are selected. The compiler&#x27;s subsequent application of the incident remains commentary, with each language&#x27;s supplied details unchanged.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ حَاطِبٍ، أَنَّهُ اعْتَمَرَ مَعَ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فِي رَكْبٍ فِيهِمْ عَمْرُو بْنُ الْعَاصِ وَأَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ عَرَّسَ بِبَعْضِ الطَّرِيقِ قَرِيبًا مِنْ بَعْضِ الْمِيَاهِ فَاحْتَلَمَ عُمَرُ وَقَدْ كَادَ أَنْ يُصْبِحَ فَلَمْ يَجِدْ مَعَ الرَّكْبِ مَاءً فَرَكِبَ حَتَّى جَاءَ الْمَاءَ فَجَعَلَ يَغْسِلُ مَا رَأَى مِنْ ذَلِكَ الاِحْتِلاَمِ حَتَّى أَسْفَرَ فَقَالَ لَهُ عَمْرُو بْنُ الْعَاصِ أَصْبَحْتَ وَمَعَنَا ثِيَابٌ فَدَعْ ثَوْبَكَ يُغْسَلُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَاعَجَبًا لَكَ يَا عَمْرُو بْنَ الْعَاصِ لَئِنْ كُنْتَ تَجِدُ ثِيَابًا أَفَكُلُّ النَّاسِ يَجِدُ ثِيَابًا وَاللَّهِ لَوْ فَعَلْتُهَا لَكَانَتْ سُنَّةً بَلْ أَغْسِلُ مَا رَأَيْتُ وَأَنْضِحُ مَا لَمْ أَرَ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   114 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي رَجُلٍ وَجَدَ فِي ثَوْبِهِ أَثَرَ احْتِلاَمٍ وَلاَ يَدْرِي مَتَى كَانَ وَلاَ يَذْكُرُ شَيْئًا رَأَى فِي مَنَامِهِ قَالَ لِيَغْتَسِلْ مِنْ أَحْدَثِ نَوْمٍ نَامَهُ فَإِنْ كَانَ صَلَّى بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ النَّوْمِ فَلْيُعِدْ مَا كَانَ صَلَّى بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ النَّوْمِ مِنْ أَجْلِ أَنَّ الرَّجُلَ رُبَّمَا احْتَلَمَ وَلاَ يَرَى شَيْئًا وَيَرَى وَلاَ يَحْتَلِمُ فَإِذَا وَجَدَ فِي ثَوْبِهِ مَاءً فَعَلَيْهِ الْغُسْلُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ عُمَرَ أَعَادَ مَا كَانَ صَلَّى لآخِرِ نَوْمٍ نَامَهُ وَلَمْ يُعِدْ مَا كَانَ قَبْلَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion); Amr ibn al-As (companion). The narrated journey and exchange between Umar and Amr are selected. The compiler&#x27;s subsequent application of the incident remains commentary, with each language&#x27;s supplied details unchanged.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father fromYahyaibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Ha ib that he had set off for Mumra with Umar ibn al-Khattab in a party of riders, among whom was Amr ibn al-As. Umar ibn al-Khattab dismounted for a rest late at night on a certain road near a certain oasis. Umar had a wet dream when it was almost dawn and there was no water among the riding party. He rode until he came to some water and then he began to wash off what he saw of the semen until it had gone. Amr ibn al-As said to him, &quot;It is morning and there are clothes with us, so allow your garment to be washed. &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab said to him, &quot;I am surprised at you, Amr ibn al-As! Even if you could find clothes, would everybody be able to find them? By Allah, if I were to do it, it would become a sunna. No, I wash what I see, and I sprinkle with water what I do not see.&quot; </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   114 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik spoke about a man who found traces of a wet dream on his clothes and did not know when it had occurred and did not remember anything he had seen in his sleep. He said, &quot;Let the intention of his ghusl be from the time when he last slept, and if he has prayed since that last sleep he should repeat it. This is because often a man has a wet dream and sees nothing, and often he sees something but does not have an emission. But, if he finds liquid on his garment he must do ghusl. This is because Umar repeated what he had prayed after the time he had last slept and not what was before it</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 114]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 114</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   114 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन हातिब से रिवायत है के उन्होंने उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के साथ उमराह किया, काफिले में अम्र बिन आस (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) भी थे। रात को हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) पानी के करीब उतरे तो उन्हें एहतलाम हो गया, सुबह करीब थी और काफिले में पानी न था। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) सवारी पर पानी के पास आए और कपड़े धोने लगे यहां तक के रोशनी हो गई। अम्र बिन आस (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: सुबह हो गई है, हमारे पास कपड़े हैं, अपना कपड़ा छोड़ दीजिये, वो धोया जाएगा और हमारे कपड़ों में से एक पहन लीजिये। उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: अजीब बात है ऐ अम्र बिन आस, क्या तुम्हारे पास कपड़े हैं तो तुम समझते हो के सब आदमियों के पास कपड़े होंगे? अल्लाह की कसम, अगर मैं ऐसा करूँ तो यह सुन्नत बन जाएगी, बल्के मैं वही धोता हूँ जहां नजासत मालूम होती है और जहां मालूम नहीं होती वहां पानी छिड़क देता हूँ।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عمر بن خطاب (companion); عمرو بن عاص (companion). The narrated journey and exchange between Umar and Amr are selected. The compiler&#x27;s subsequent application of the incident remains commentary, with each language&#x27;s supplied details unchanged.</p>

<p dir="rtl">یحیی بن عبداللہ بن حاطب سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے عمرہ کیا ساتھ عمر بن خطاب سے کئی شتر سواروں میں ان میں عمرو بن عاص بھی تھے اور عمر بن خطاب رات کو اترے قریب پانی کے تو احتلام ہوا حضرت عمر کو اور صبح قریب تھی اور قافلہ میں پانی نہ تھا تو سوار ہو کے حضرت عمر یہاں تک کہ آئے پانی کے پاس اور دھونے لگے کپڑے اپنے یہاں تک کہ روشنی ہوگئی اور عمر بن عاص نے کہا حضرت عمر سے صبح ہوگئی ہمارے پاس کپڑے ہیں اپنا کپڑا چھوڑ دیجئے دھو ڈالا جائے گا اور ہمارے کپڑوں میں سے ایک کپڑا پہن لیجئے تو کہا عمر بن خطاب نے کہ تعجب ہے اے عمرو بن عاص کیا تمہارے پاس کپڑے ہیں تو تم سمجھتے ہو کہ سب آدمیوں کے پاس کپڑے ہوں گے قسم اللہ کی اگر میں ایسا کروں تو یہ امر سنت ہو جائے بلکہ دھو ڈالتا ہوں میں یہاں نجاست معلوم ہوتی ہے اور پانی چھڑک دیتا ہوں جہاں نہیں معلوم ہوتی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 114]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya bin Abdullah bin Hatib se riwayat hai ke unhone Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke saath umrah kiya, kafile mein Amr bin Aas (Radi Allahu Anhu) bhi thay. Raat ko Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) pani ke qareeb utre toh unhein ihtilam ho gaya, subah qareeb thi aur kafile mein pani na tha. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) sawari par pani ke paas aaye aur kapde dhone lage yahan tak ke roshni ho gayi. Amr bin Aas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Subah ho gayi hai, hamare paas kapde hain, apna kapda chhor dijiye, woh dhoya jayega aur hamare kapdon mein se ek pehan lijiye. Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Ajeeb baat hai Aey Amr bin Aas, kya tumhare paas kapde hain toh tum samajhte ho ki sab aadmiyon ke paas kapde honge? Allah ki qasam, agar main aisa karoon toh yeh sunnat ban jayegi, balkay main wahi dhota hoon jahan najasat maloom hoti hai aur jahan maloom nahi hoti wahan pani chhirak deta hoon.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/114" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 115

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 115]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّ أُمَّ سُلَيْمٍ، قَالَتْ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْمَرْأَةُ تَرَى فِي الْمَنَامِ مِثْلَ مَا يَرَى الرَّجُلُ أَتَغْتَسِلُ فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ نَعَمْ فَلْتَغْتَسِلْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ لَهَا عَائِشَةُ أُفٍّ لَكِ وَهَلْ تَرَى ذَلِكَ الْمَرْأَةُ فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ تَرِبَتْ يَمِينُكِ وَمِنْ أَيْنَ يَكُونُ الشَّبَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah answering Umm Sulayman and Aisha. The complete dialogue about a womans dream and ghusl was read. The explicit liquid gloss remains explanatory context. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr that Umm Sulayman said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;Should a woman do ghusl when she experiences the same as a man in her sleep?&quot; The Messenger of Al lah said to her, &quot;Yes, she should do ghusl. &quot;A&#x27;isha said to her, &quot;Shame on you! Does a woman see that?&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to her, &quot;May your right hand be full of dust. From where does family resemblance come?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 115]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उरवाह बिन जुबैर से रिवायत है के उम्मे सुलैम (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा: अगर औरत ख्वाब में वैसा ही देखे जैसा मर्द देखता है, तो क्या उस पर गुस्ल है? आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) ने उम्मे सुलैम से कहा: तेरी शर्म जाए! क्या औरत भी ख्वाब देखती है? तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: तेरा दाहिना हाथ खाक-आलूद हो (ताज्जुब है), और वहां (औलाद) की मुशाबेहत कहां से होती है?</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Zubair se riwayat hai ke Umme Sulaim (Radi Allahu Anha) ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha: Agar aurat khwab mein waisa hi dekhe jaisa mard dekhta hai, toh kya us par ghusl hai? Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) ne Umme Sulaim se kaha: Teri sharam jaye! Kya aurat bhi khwab dekhti hai? Toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Tera daahna hath khak-aalud ho (taajjub hai), aur wahan (aulad) ki mushabehat kahan se hoti hai?</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/115" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 116

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A116)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ زَيْنَبَ بِنْتِ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ جَاءَتْ أُمُّ سُلَيْمٍ امْرَأَةُ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَتْ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنَّ اللَّهَ لاَ يَسْتَحْيِي مِنَ الْحَقِّ هَلْ عَلَى الْمَرْأَةِ مِنْ غُسْلٍ إِذَا هِيَ احْتَلَمَتْ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ نَعَمْ إِذَا رَأَتِ الْمَاءَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 116]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/116" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   116 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father from Zaynab bint Abi Salama that Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Umm Salama, the wife of Abu Talha al-Ansari, came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! Allah is not ashamed of the truth-does a woman have to do ghusl if she has had an erotic dream?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Yes, if she sees any liquid</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 116]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/116" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मे सलमा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के उम्मे सुलैम (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आईं और कहा: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) क्या औरत पर भी ग़ुस्ल फ़र्ज़ है अगर उसे एह्तिलाम हो? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: हाँ (मणि) देख ले.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 116]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/116" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام سلمہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے ام سلیم آئیں رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس تو کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نہیں شرماتا اللہ سچ سے کیا عورت پر بھی غسل ہے جو اس کو احتلام ہو فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ہاں جب کہ دیکھے پانی کو ۔</p>

<p dir="ltr">Umme Salama (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Umme Sulaim (Radi Allahu Anha) Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aayin aur kaha: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), Allah sach batane mein sharmata nahi, kya aurat par bhi ghusl farz hai agar use ihtilam ho? ## Narration 117

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A117)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). The statement is attributed to Abdullah ibn Umar. Explicit definitional parentheses are separately preserved where present.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ لاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يُغْتَسَلَ بِفَضْلِ الْمَرْأَةِ مَا لَمْ تَكُنْ حَائِضًا أَوْ جُنُبًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 117]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/117" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The statement is attributed to Abdullah ibn Umar. Explicit definitional parentheses are separately preserved where present.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;There is no harm in doing ghusl with water that has been used by one&#x27;s wife as long as she is not menstruating or in a state of major ritual impurity </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 117]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/117" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 119

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A119)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). The opening source account concerning Ibn Umar&#x27;s household is selected. The subsequent compiler rulings remain commentary, along with the Bengali definition of khumra.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَغْسِلُ جَوَارِيهِ رِجْلَيْهِ وَيُعْطِينَهُ الْخُمْرَةَ وَهُنَّ حُيَّضٌ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 119]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/119" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 119</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   119 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ رَجُلٍ لَهُ نِسْوَةٌ وَجَوَارِي هَلْ يَطَؤُهُنَّ جَمِيعًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَغْتَسِلَ فَقَالَ لاَ بَأْسَ بِأَنْ يُصِيبَ الرَّجُلُ جَارِيَتَيْهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَغْتَسِلَ فَأَمَّا النِّسَاءُ الْحَرَائِرُ فَيُكْرَهُ أَنْ يُصِيبَ الرَّجُلُ الْمَرْأَةَ الْحُرَّةَ فِي يَوْمِ الأُخْرَى فَأَمَّا أَنَّ يُصِيبَ الْجَارِيَةَ ثُمَّ يُصِيبَ الأُخْرَى وَهُوَ جُنُبٌ فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ رَجُلٍ جُنُبٍ وُضِعَ لَهُ مَاءٌ يَغْتَسِلُ بِهِ فَسَهَا فَأَدْخَلَ أَصْبُعَهُ فِيهِ لِيَعْرِفَ حَرَّ الْمَاءِ مِنْ بَرْدِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ إِنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ أَصَابَ أَصْبُعَهُ أَذًى فَلاَ أَرَى ذَلِكَ يُنَجِّسُ عَلَيْهِ الْمَاءَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/119" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The opening source account concerning Ibn Umar&#x27;s household is selected. The subsequent compiler rulings remain commentary, along with the Bengali definition of khumra.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that the slave girls of Abdullah ibn Umar used to wash his feet and bring him a mat of palm leaves while they were menstruating. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 119]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/119" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 119</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   119 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik was asked whether a man who had women and slavegirlscould have intercourse with all of them before he did ghusl. He said, &quot;There is no harm in a man having intercourse with two of his slave girls before he does ghusl. It is disapproved of, however, to go to a freewoman on another&#x27;s day. There is no harm in making love first to one slave girl and then to another when one is junub.&quot; Malik was asked about a man who was junub and water was put down for him to do ghusl with.Then he forgot and put his finger into it to find out whether it was hot or cold. Malik said, &quot;If no filth has soiled his fingers, I do not consider that that makes the water impure</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/119" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<p dir="rtl">ابن عمر کی لونڈیاں ان کے پاؤں دھوتی تھیں اور ان کو جائے نماز اٹھا کر دیتی تھیں حالت حیض میں ۔</p>

## Narration 120

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A120)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ خَرَجْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي بَعْضِ أَسْفَارِهِ حَتَّى إِذَا كُنَّا بِالْبَيْدَاءِ - أَوْ بِذَاتِ الْجَيْشِ - انْقَطَعَ عِقْدٌ لِي فَأَقَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى الْتِمَاسِهِ وَأَقَامَ النَّاسُ مَعَهُ وَلَيْسُوا عَلَى مَاءٍ وَلَيْسَ مَعَهُمْ مَاءٌ فَأَتَى النَّاسُ إِلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَقَالُوا أَلاَ تَرَى مَا صَنَعَتْ عَائِشَةُ أَقَامَتْ بِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَبِالنَّاسِ وَلَيْسُوا عَلَى مَاءٍ وَلَيْسَ مَعَهُمْ مَاءٌ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ فَجَاءَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَاضِعٌ رَأْسَهُ عَلَى فَخِذِي قَدْ نَامَ فَقَالَ حَبَسْتِ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَالنَّاسَ وَلَيْسُوا عَلَى مَاءٍ وَلَيْسَ مَعَهُمْ مَاءٌ قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ فَعَاتَبَنِي أَبُو بَكْرٍ فَقَالَ مَا شَاءَ اللَّهُ أَنْ يَقُولَ وَجَعَلَ يَطْعُنُ بِيَدِهِ فِي خَاصِرَتِي فَلاَ يَمْنَعُنِي مِنَ التَّحَرُّكِ إِلاَّ مَكَانُ رَأْسِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى فَخِذِي فَنَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَتَّى أَصْبَحَ عَلَى غَيْرِ مَاءٍ فَأَنْزَلَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى آيَةَ التَّيَمُّمِ فَتَيَمَّمُوا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أُسَيْدُ بْنُ حُضَيْرٍ مَا هِيَ بِأَوَّلِ بَرَكَتِكُمْ يَا آلَ أَبِي بَكْرٍ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَبَعَثْنَا الْبَعِيرَ الَّذِي كُنْتُ عَلَيْهِ فَوَجَدْنَا الْعِقْدَ تَحْتَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd arRahman ibn al-Qasim from his father that A&#x27;isha umm al-muminin said, &quot;We went out on a journey with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and, when we came to Bayda&#x27; or Dhat al-Jaysh, a necklace of mine broke. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, stopped to look for it and the people stopped with him. There was no water nearby and the people were not carrying any with them, so they came to Abu Bakr as-Siddiq and said, &#x27;Don&#x27;t you see what A&#x27;isha has done? She has made the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and the people stop when there is no water nearby and they are not carrying any with them.&#x27; &quot; A&#x27;isha continued, &quot;Abu Bakr came and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, had fallen asleep with his head on my thigh . Abu Bakr said, &#x27;You have made the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and the people stop when there is no water nearby and they are not carrying any with them &#x27; &quot; She continued, &quot;Abu Bakr remonstrated with me and said whatever Allah willed him to say, and began to poke me in the waist. The only thing that stopped me from moving was that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, had his head on my thigh. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, slept until morning found him with no water. Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, sent down the ayat of tayammum and so they did tayammum. Usayd ibn Hudayr said, &#x27;This is not the first baraka from you, O family of Abu Bakr.&#x27;&quot; A&#x27;isha added, &quot;We roused the camel I had been on and found the necklace under it.&quot; Malik was asked whether a man who did tayammum for one prayer should do tayammum when the time of the next prayer came or whether the first tayammum was enough. He said, &quot;No, he does tayammum for every prayer, because he has to look for water for every prayer. If he looks for it and does not find it then he does tayammum.&quot; Malik was asked whether a man who did tayammum could lead others in prayer if they were in wudu. He said, &quot;I prefer that someone else should lead them. However, I see no harm in it if he does lead them in prayer.&quot; Yahya said that Malik said that a man who did tayammum because he could not find any water, and then stood and said the takbir and entered into the prayer, and then someone came with some water, did not stop his prayer but completed it with tayammum and did wudu for future prayers. Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;Whoever rises for prayer and does not find water and so does what Allah has ordered him to do of tayammum has obeyed Allah. Someone who does find water is neither purer than him nor more perfect in prayer, because both have been commanded and each does as Allah has commanded. What Allah has commanded as far as wudu is concerned is for the one who finds water, and tayammum is for the one who does not find water before he enters into the prayer.&quot; Malik said that a man who was in a state of major ritual impurity could do tayammum and read his portion of Qur&#x27;an and do voluntary prayers as long as he did not find any water. This applied only to circumstances in which it was allowable to pray with tayammum</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के हम एक सफर में रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ निकले। जब हम बैदा या ज़ात-उल-जैश पहुंचे तो मेरा हार टूट कर गिर गया। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) उसे ढूंढने के लिए रुक गए और लोग भी आपके साथ रुक गए, वहां पानी न था। लोग अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आए और कहा: देखा तुमने आयशा ने क्या किया, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) और लोगों को रोक दिया और यहां पानी नहीं है। अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) मेरे पास आए, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मेरा सर अपनी रान पर रखे सो रहे थे। अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: तुमने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को रोक दिया और पानी भी नहीं है। आयशा ने कहा: अबू बक्र मुझ पर गुस्सा हुए और अपने हाथ से मेरी कोख में मारने लगे, मैं हिल जाती मगर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का सर मुबारक मेरी रान पर था इसलिए मैं हिल न सकी। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) सुबह तक सोते रहे, पानी न था तो अल्लाह तआला ने तयम्मुम की आयत नाज़िल फरमा दी। तब उसैद बिन हुदै़र ने कहा: ऐ अबू बक्र के घर वालों, यह तुम्हारी पहली बरकत नहीं है। आयशा ने कहा: जब हम चलने लगे तो वो हार उस ऊंट के नीचे से निकला जिस पर हम सवार थे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عائشہ سے روایت ہے کہ ہم نکلے ساتھ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے کسی سفر میں تو جب پہنچے ہم بیدا یا ذات الجیش کو گلوبند میرا ٹوٹ کر گر پڑا تو ٹھہر گئے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اس کے ڈھونڈنے کے لئے اور لوگ بھی ٹھہر گئے ساتھ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے اور وہاں پانی نہ تھا اور نہ ساتھ لوگوں کے پانی تھا تب لوگ آئے ابوبکر صدیق کے پاس اور کہا کہ دیکھا تم نے کیا عائشہ نے ٹھہرا دیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو اور لوگوں کو اور نہ یہاں پانی ہے نہ ہمارے ساتھ پانی ہے تو ابوبکر آئے میرے پاس اور رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اپنا سر میری ران پر رکھے ہوئے سو رہے تھے تو کہا ابوبکر نے روک دیا تو نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اور لوگوں کو اور نہ پانی ملتا ہے نہ ان کے پاس پانی ہے کہا عائشہ نے غصہ ہوئے میرے اوپر ابوبکر اور اپنے ہاتھ سے میری کوکھ میں مارنے لگے تو میں ہل جاتی مگر رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کا سر مبارک میری ران پر تھا اس وجہ سے میں نہ ہل سکتی تھی پس سوتے رہے آنحضرت صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم یہاں تک کہ صبح ہوئی اور پانی نہ تھا تو اتاری اللہ جل جلالہ نے آیت تیمم کی تب کہا اسی دن اسید بن الحضیر نے کہا اے ابوبکر کے گھر والوں یہ کچھ تمہاری پہلی برکت نہیں ہے یعنی تم سے ہمیشہ ایسی ہی برکتیں اور راحتیں مسلمانوں کو حاصل ہوئی ہیں کہا عائشہ نے جب ہم چلنے لگے تو وہ گلو بن اس اونٹ کے نیچے سے نکلا جس پر ہم سوار تھے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke hum ek safar mein Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath nikle. Jab hum Baida ya Zaat-ul-Jaish pahunche toh mera haar toot kar gir gaya. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) use dhoondne ke liye ruk gaye aur log bhi aapke saath ruk gaye, wahan pani na tha. Log Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaye aur kaha: Dekha tumne Ayesha ne kya kiya, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aur logon ko rok diya aur yahan pani nahi hai. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) mere paas aaye, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) mera sar apni raan par rakhe so rahe thay. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Tumne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko rok diya aur pani bhi nahi hai. Ayesha ne kaha: Abu Bakr mujh par gussa huye aur apne hath se meri kokh mein marne lage, main hil jati magar Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ka sar mubarak meri raan par tha isliye main hil na saki. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) subah tak sote rahe, pani na tha toh Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne tayammum ki ayat nazil farma di. Tab Usaid bin Hudair ne kaha: Aey Abu Bakr ke ghar walon, yeh tumhari pehli barkat nahi hai. Ayesha ne kaha: Jab hum chalne lage toh woh haar us oont ke neeche se nikla jis par hum sawar thay.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 120]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/120" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
