## Narration 121

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A121)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ (companion). The narrated journey and Ibn Umar&#x27;s tayammum are preserved. Place names and the source&#x27;s direction of travel are not harmonized with another language.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّهُ أَقْبَلَ هُوَ وَعَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ مِنَ الْجُرُفِ حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَا بِالْمِرْبَدِ نَزَلَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ فَتَيَمَّمَ صَعِيدًا طَيِّبًا فَمَسَحَ وَجْهَهُ وَيَدَيْهِ إِلَى الْمِرْفَقَيْنِ ثُمَّ صَلَّى ‏.‏</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The narrated journey and Ibn Umar&#x27;s tayammum are preserved. Place names and the source&#x27;s direction of travel are not harmonized with another language.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar and he were approaching Juruf. When they got to Mirbad, Abdullah got down and did tayammum with some good earth. He wiped his face, and his arms to the elbows, and then prayed</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 121]</p>

## Narration 122

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A122)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ (companion). Ibn Umar&#x27;s reported practice is selected; Malik&#x27;s subsequent explanatory legal answer is retained as commentary where supplied.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَتَيَمَّمُ إِلَى الْمِرْفَقَيْنِ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 122]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/122" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ كَيْفَ التَّيَمُّمُ وَأَيْنَ يَبْلُغُ بِهِ فَقَالَ يَضْرِبُ ضَرْبَةً لِلْوَجْهِ وَضَرْبَةً لِلْيَدَيْنِ وَيَمْسَحُهُمَا إِلَى الْمِرْفَقَيْنِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 122]</p>

## Narration 123

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A123)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ (successor). The initial narrated answer belongs to Said ibn al-Musayyab. Subsequent compiler legal discussion and its Quran citation remain source context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ حَرْمَلَةَ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، سَأَلَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ الْجُنُبِ، يَتَيَمَّمُ ثُمَّ يُدْرِكُ الْمَاءَ فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ إِذَا أَدْرَكَ الْمَاءَ فَعَلَيْهِ الْغُسْلُ لِمَا يُسْتَقْبَلُ ‏.‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 123]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 123</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   123 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِيمَنِ احْتَلَمَ وَهُوَ فِي سَفَرٍ وَلاَ يَقْدِرُ مِنَ الْمَاءِ إِلاَّ عَلَى قَدْرِ الْوُضُوءِ وَهُوَ لاَ يَعْطَشُ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَ الْمَاءَ قَالَ يَغْسِلُ بِذَلِكَ فَرْجَهُ وَمَا أَصَابَهُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ الأَذَى ثُمَّ يَتَيَمَّمُ صَعِيدًا طَيِّبًا كَمَا أَمَرَهُ اللَّهُ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ رَجُلٍ جُنُبٍ أَرَادَ أَنْ يَتَيَمَّمَ فَلَمْ يَجِدْ تُرَابًا إِلاَّ تُرَابَ سَبَخَةٍ هَلْ يَتَيَمَّمُ بِالسِّبَاخِ وَهَلْ تُكْرَهُ الصَّلاَةُ فِي السِّبَاخِ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ بَأْسَ بِالصَّلاَةِ فِي السِّبَاخِ وَالتَّيَمُّمِ مِنْهَا لأَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ ‏{‏فَتَيَمَّمُوا صَعِيدًا طَيِّبًا‏}‏ فَكُلُّ مَا كَانَ صَعِيدًا فَهُوَ يُتَيَمَّمُ بِهِ سِبَاخًا كَانَ أَوْ غَيْرَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The initial narrated answer belongs to Said ibn al-Musayyab. Subsequent compiler legal discussion and its Quran citation remain source context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd arRahman ibn Harmala that a man asked Said ibn al-Musayyab about what a man who was junub and had done tayammum should do when he came across water. Said said, &quot;When he comes across water he must do ghusl for what comes after.&quot; </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 123]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 123</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   123 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said about some one who had a wet dream while he was on a journey and there was only enough water for wudu and he was not thirsty and so he did not need to use it for that, &quot;Let him wash his genitals, and whatever the semen has fallen on, with the water and then he does tayammum with good earth as Allah has ordered him.&quot; Malik was asked whether a man who was junub and wished to do tayammum but could only find salty earth could do tayammum with that earth, and whether it was disapproved of to pray on salty earth. He said, &quot;There is no harm in praying on salty earth or in using it to do tayammum, because Allah the Blessed and Exalted has said, &#x27;...and do tayammum with good earth.&#x27; One is purified by tayammum with everything that is earth, whether it is salty or otherwise</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/123" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर रहमान बिन हरमला से रिवायत है कि एक शख़्स ने सईद बिन मुसय्यब से पूछा कि जनाबी (जिस पर ग़ुस्ल फ़र्ज़ हो) ने तयम्मुम किया और फिर बाद में पानी पा लिया, तो सईद ने कहा कि अगर जनाबी पानी पा ले तो उस पर ग़ुस्ल वाजिब हो जाएगा।</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: سعید بن مسیب (successor). The initial narrated answer belongs to Said ibn al-Musayyab. Subsequent compiler legal discussion and its Quran citation remain source context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالرحمن بن حرملہ سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے پوچھا سعید بن مسیب سے کہ جنبی نے تیمم کیا پھر پایا پانی کو تو کہا سعید نے کہ جنبی پائے پانی تو اس پر غسل واجب ہوگا آئندہ کے واسطے ۔</p>

## Narration 124

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A124)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، سَأَلَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ مَا يَحِلُّ لِي مِنِ امْرَأَتِي وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لِتَشُدَّ عَلَيْهَا إِزَارَهَا ثُمَّ شَأْنَكَ بِأَعْلاَهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 124]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/124" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 124</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   124 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that a man questioned the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, saying, &quot;What is permitted me from my wife when she is menstruating?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Let her wrap her waist-wrapper round herself tightly, and then what is above that is your concern</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 124]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/124" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलाम से रिवायत है की एक शख्स ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा कि क्या मेरे लिए अपनी बीवी से हमबिस्तरी करना दुरूस्त है जब वह हैज़ा (हैज़ की हालत में) हो? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: उस पर तहबन्द (इज़ार) बांधो</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 124]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/124" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے پوچھا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہا کیا درست ہے مجھ کو اپنی عورت سے جب وہ حائضہ ہو تو فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے باندھ اس پر تہ بند اس کے پھر تجھے اختیار ہے تہ بند کے اوپر ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 124]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ki ek shakhs ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha ki kya mere liye apni biwi se hambistari karna durust hai jab woh haiza (haiz ki halat mein) ho? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Us par tehband (izar) baandho, phir uske upar (baki hisse se) tumhe ikhtiyar hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 124]</p>

## Narration 125

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A125)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 2</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَتْ مُضْطَجِعَةً مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي ثَوْبٍ وَاحِدٍ وَأَنَّهَا قَدْ وَثَبَتْ وَثْبَةً شَدِيدَةً فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ مَا لَكِ لَعَلَّكِ نَفِسْتِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ يَعْنِي الْحَيْضَةَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ شُدِّي عَلَى نَفْسِكِ إِزَارَكِ ثُمَّ عُودِي إِلَى مَضْجَعِكِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman that on one occasion A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was sleeping with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, in one garment, when suddenly she jumped up sharply. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to her, &quot;What&#x27;s the matter with you? Are you losing blood?&quot;, meaning menstruating. She said, &quot;Yes.&quot; He said, &quot;Wrap your waist-wrapper tightly about you, and return to your sleeping- place</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">राबिया बिन अबी अब्दुर रहमान से रिवायत है कि हज़रत आइशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ एक कपड़े में लेटी हुई थीं, इतने में वो कूद कर अलग हो गईं। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: शायद तुम्हें हैज़ (periods) आ गया है? उन्होंने कहा: जी हाँ। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अपना तहबंद बांधो और फिर वापस आ कर लेट जाओ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن ابی عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ حضرت عائشہ لیٹی تھیں ساتھ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ایک کپڑے میں اتنے میں کود کر الگ ہو گئیں تو فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے شائد حیض آیا تجھ کو کہا ہاں تو فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے باندھ لے تہ بند اپنے پھر آ کو وہیں لیٹ جا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abi Abdur Rahman se riwayat hai ki Hazrat Aishah (Radi Allahu Anha) Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath ek kapde mein leti hui thin, itne mein woh kood kar alag ho gayin. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Shayad tumhe haiz (periods) aa gaya hai? Unhone kaha: Ji haan. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Apna tehband baandho aur phir wapas aa kar let jao.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 125]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/125" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
